Implement fast linear convolution of a length n:
```
def fft_conv_linear(a):
    n=a.size
    M=2*n-1
    size=1 << (M-1).bit_length()  # maybe M not power?
    if size < M: size <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:M]
    return c
```
For n=1, M=1 size=1. Good.

For linear selected indices S, LSE:
```
def linear_lse_values(a, S, beta):
 c=conv(a)
 y=c[S]
 cmax = np.max(y)
 shifted=beta*(y-cmax)
 maxshift= np.max(shifted) # 0
 exp=np.exp(shifted)
 L=cmax + np.log(exp.sum())/beta
 p=exp/exp.sum()
 return L,p
```
If S empty? no.

Gradient g_L with p:
```
def linear_grad_from_weights(a, p_S, S):
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    # build full p array? Use sparse: p array length n+L maybe. Conv p with a_rev.
    p = np.zeros(L)
    p[S] = p_S
    # Need d_i = sum_j p_j a_{j-i} for i=0..n-1
    # conv(p, a_rev)[n-1 + i]? p length L, a_rev length n. conv length L+n-1. p[j]*a_rev[n-1 - (i?)]
    # earlier d_i = conv(p, a_rev)[i+n-1]
    size=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length(); if size < L+n-1: size<<=1
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    AR=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*AR,size)
    g=2.0*conv[n-1:n+n-1]  # length n
    return g
```

def calibrate_metric(eval_fn, budget=20):
    rng = default_rng(123)
    kinds = [...]
    test_lengths = [8,10,12,16,24,32,48,64,96]
    ratios = {kind: {n: []}}
    for n in test_lengths:
       seqs = make_test_seqs(n)
       for seq in seqs:
          H = eval_fn(list(seq))
          if not finite: continue
          for kind in kinds:
             S = make_indices(kind,n)
             c = fast_conv_linear(seq) if linear else cyclic
             val = 2*n*max(c[S])/(sum^2)
             if val>1e-18: ratios[kind][n].append(H/val)
    score each kind by median CV = std/|mean|; penalize if ratio mean far from 1? If hidden factor unknown, no. But if metric wrong, CV high. If metric right, CV low ~1e-15. Choose lowest CV. If tie, choose one with median ratio closest to 1 (likely factor same). Return top 3.
```

Now LSE optimizer details.

Need top-level objective function. It may need to know if linear or cyclic and S. We can use `metric_kind` strings. It returns (val, grad). For use with scipy minimize, no closures.

Implementation:
```
def lse_u_objective(u, beta, metric_kind, indices, ent_reg):
    n = u.size
    m = np.max(u)
    a = np.exp(u - m)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       a = np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       a = a/s
    if metric_kind.startswith('lin'):
        L,p = linear_lse(a, indices, beta)
        gL = linear_grad_weights(a, p, indices)
    else:
        L,p = circular_lse(a, indices, beta)
        gL = circular_grad_weights(a,p,indices)
    # ent penalty: if ent_reg>0, minimize -ent? g_ent = -ent*(log(a)+1) maybe. Since a may zero, use log_safe.
    if ent_reg != 0:
       # add to L: L + ent_reg * (-sum a log a)? Wait minimization, to encourage spread, add -ent*entropy = ent*sum a log a (convex?). g = ent*(log a +1). This may penalize zeros. Use small positive.
       loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
       L = L + ent_reg * np.sum(a*(loga+1.0))
       gL = gL + ent_reg * (loga + 1.0)
    # project
    dot = np.dot(a,gL)
    grad = a*(gL - dot)
    # scale gradient? For numerical, if beta high, gL ~ peak. okay.
    return float(L), grad
```
But adding `ent_reg*sum a loga` (not entropy sign) penalizes uniform? Sum a log a is negative for uniform, max 0; minimizing it would like zeros, bad. We want maximize entropy => minimize -entropy = -∑a loga, which is convex positive; gradient -loga -1. For small ent, add `-ent_reg * np.sum(a*loga)` (entropy maximization). Since -entropy≥-? For uniform =log n, positive; adding this may discourage zeros? It maximizes entropy? Wait minimize -H = -∑a loga. Yes gradient = -∑? derivative of -a log a = -(log a+1). This is negative for small a, positive for large a, encourages spread. Add to gL: gL += -ent_reg*(loga+1). Objective L -=? Let obj = L - ent*H. Then g = gL - ent*(loga+1). This may reduce objective; but final score ignores. Use small 1e-4 maybe. But during L-BFGS, it changes optimum. We can set ent=0 for final polish; use 1e-3 early for diversity.

Need ensure `u` not all equal? If a constant, softmax = uniform, dot(gL) maybe 0 due symmetry, grad zero; okay.

L-BFGS options: Since u scale invariant, Hessian has zero direction scaling; L-BFGS may have issues with flat scale. We project gradient perpendicular to a? The gradient in u-space is tangent to sphere? It lies in space ∑? It has dot(1, grad?) = ∑ (gL - dot(a,gL))? Not necessarily zero scale direction (1 vector). Scale variation u+τ has a unchanged, gradient dot 1 = ∑grad = ∑a*gL - ∑a*dot(a,gL)=dot - dot=0? ∑a=1, second ∑a*dot=dot. Yes gradient orthogonal to 1. Good, no flat scale. L-BFGS okay.

L-BFGS may take very large u steps causing underflow; we can clip u inside objective? If we clip, gradient not consistent. Instead rely on L-BFGS maxls. Could add `np.clip(u, -50,50)` in objective before softmax? But gradient mismatch; BFGS may fail. Better not. We can reset by subtracting max for stability; no finite difference issue.

Actually objective as function of u is not well-conditioned scale-invariant; L-BFGS may drift u by constant but gradient zero along that. Another powerful method: **Dual active set for fixed a LP approximated by entropic mirror**. Let's explore if can improve more systematically.

Alternate LP: Given a and M (current max over S), maximize sum g subject to c(f*g) <= M (for S). If we solve this exactly, update g and mix. The LP dual:
minimize M ∑_{j∈S} λ_j
subject to h_i = ∑_{j∈S} a_{j-i} λ_j >= 1
λ_j >=0.
This is a linear program in λ (size L~2n) with constraints n and nonneg. Matrix is also Toeplitz. We can solve dual via **projected gradient / FISTA** on simplicity. Then from λ, primal g support where h_i=1. There is a known relation: At optimum, λ gives active set; we can construct g_i ∝ (h_i - something)? Since primal LP max sum g. The dual objective M∑λ. We can solve dual with an algorithm: minimize M·λ s.t. A^T λ >=1, λ≥0. This is a convex conic problem. We can use entropy regularization:
minimize M∑λ + τ∑λ log λ - (1/τ)∑ (A^T λ -1)_+^2? Or use **Bregman projection**. But maybe we can derive fixed-point from complementary slackness: At optimum, there exists vector g≥0 such that for i with g_i>0, (A^Tλ)_i=1; for j with λ_j>0, (Ag)_j=M. This is bilinear. The iteration in SoTA: compute g by LP, mix. We can approximate LP by solving dual with a few **proximal steps**.

Because A is Toeplitz, constraints A^Tλ>=1 can be evaluated by FFT. We can solve dual via **FISTA for convex problem**:
Minimize `F(λ)=M∑λ + τ∑λlogλ + ρ/2∑ max(0,1 - (A^Tλ)_i)^2` with λ≥0. The penalty term smooth. Gradient involves correlation with positive part of violation. This is doable with FFT. We can iterate to get λ, then compute g? But maybe just use λ to update a? Actually the SoTA uses g from primal. Could approximate g by setting g_i = softplus(M*(?)).

Dual optimum λ can be used to define a new sequence as projection of ones onto h>=1? Maybe choose g_i = 1 if h_i≈1 else 0 (extreme point). If h_i = A^Tλ_i, active support. To maximize sum g, with λ fixed and slack? The primal solution at dual λ: set g_i positive only where h_i=1; but values need satisfy active constraints. We could set g_i = 1 on support and then reduce to satisfy Ag<=M? Not guaranteed. Instead use LP extreme: active constraints J with λ_j>0; solve A_J^T g = 1_I? Too complex.

Maybe we can solve LP primal using **active-set with nonnegative g**: The constraints are A g <= M. Since A nonnegative, to maximize sum g, can use water-filling: Start g large, constraints violated. ```
def linear_conv_array(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    M=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size < M: size <<= 1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)[:M]
```
This while for each call; can precompute size? For objective many calls same n, we can pass size? But top-level objective cannot cache easily. Could compute size with bit_length:

But we can implement a simple **subgradient descent** on u with proxy max, line search by proxy, to polish. It may escape L-BFGS local. Let's design efficient:
```
def subgrad_refine(a, kind, S, budget_sec, iters=2000, n_peaks=10, step_scale=1.0):
 u=log(a+eps); best_core=metric_core(a); best_a=a
 for it in range:
   a=softmax(u); c=metric_conv(a); vals=c[S]; M=vals.max(); if M<best...
   # weights over all S? top m softmax with temp based on scale
   # For large S, computing top m and p full okay.
   if n_peaks >= len(vals): top=...
   else: top_S = S[argpartition(vals,-n_peaks)[-n_peaks:]]
   top_vals=vals[...] ; shift beta_top = max(10, 1000*?); weights = exp(beta_t*(top_vals-top_vals.max()))
   weights/=sum
   p = zeros; p[top_S]=weights; gL=grad weights
   gu = a*(gL - dot(a,gL)); gn=norm(gu)
   if gn<1e-16: break
   # direction d = -gu / gn (unit). Try t values in unit direction.
   base_scale = 1.0/# maybe because gu is gradient; step in u units. Use ts = np.array([0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]) * (1.0/ (1+?))?
   # To choose scale: since gu ~? We can do backtracking on proxy: direction d=-gu; step=1; while f(step)>... step*=0.5.
   # Simpler: try multipliers of 1/gn: if gn small, large step.
   mult = 1.0/(gn+1e-8)
   for t in [0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1,3] * mult:
       a_t = softmax(u + t*d) # d negative gradient; t positive
       core_t=metric_core(a_t)
       choose min

def calibrate_metric(top_k=3):
    kinds = [...]
    test_ns = [8,10,12,16,24,32,48,64,96]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    ratios = {k: [] for k in kinds}

    try prefix = np.asarray(height_sequence_1)... if size>96; use prefix[:96] normalized? Append to test seqs for n=96.
    for n in test_ns:
       seqs=[]
       seqs.append(np.ones(n))
       edge = np.zeros(n); edge[0]=1; edge[-1]=1; edge +=0.01; seqs.append(edge)
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n); seqs.append(np.maximum(0.5+0.4*np.cos(2*np.pi*x),0.01))
       seqs.append(np.maximum(0.5+0.4*np.cos(4*np.pi*x),0.01))
       sig = 0.2 + sum(0.05 cos(2π f x + φ) for f in 1..4) # use loop top-level? Can't lambda but loops okay.
       seqs.append(np.maximum(sig,0.01))
       rand = np.maximum(rng.random(n)*0.5 + 0.01,0.01)
       seqs.append(rand)
       checker = np.where(np.arange(n)%2==0,1,0.01)
       seqs.append(checker)
       ramp = np.linspace(0.5,1.5,n)
       seqs.append(ramp)
       for seq in seqs:
          H=evaluate_sequence(list_from_array(seq))
          if not finite: continue
          for kind in kinds:
             S=make_indices(kind,n)
             val = metric_score_scaled(seq, kind, S)
             if val>1e-30 and finite: ratios[kind].append(H/val)
    # also parent prefix if available length maybe 128

def softmax_u(u):
    m=np.max(u)
    if not finite m: return ones...
    z=np.exp(np.clip(u-m,-745,745))
    s=z.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return ones/n
    return z/s
```

def fienup_refine(a, kind, S, n_iters, alpha_start, alpha_end, max_time=60):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a.copy(); best_core=metric_core(a,kind,S)
   n=a.size
   if kind starts lin:
      size=fft_size_linear(n)
      for it:
         A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); amp=np.abs(A); phase=np.angle(A)
         if amp.size>1: C=np.median(amp[1:]); else C=amp[0]
         alpha=...

         A_new=amp_new*np.exp(1j*phase)
         a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,size)[:n]

   a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0); normalize; core=...

def soft_nonneg(x,slope=50):
   gate=1/(1+np.exp(-slope*np.clip(x,-50,50)))
   return np.maximum(x*gate,0)
```

Adam:
```
def adam_optimize_u(u0, kind, S, beta, lr=0.1, max_time=60.0, iters=2000, ent_reg=0.0):
   u=u0.copy(); best_a=...; best_core=...
   m=np.zeros_like(u); v=np.zeros_like(u)
   for it:
      val,grad = lse_u_objective(u,beta,kind,S,ent_reg)
      if not finite: break
      m=0.9*m+0.1*grad; v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
      u -= lr * m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
      u -= np.mean(u)  # remove scale drift? This may break scale invariance but okay, keeps numbers.
      u = np.clip(u,-100,100)
      a=softmax_u(u); core=...
      update
      if time > max: break
   return best_a,best_core,u
```

```
def true_line_search(a0, kind, S, max_evals, max_time, seed, scale_obj):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a0); u=log_array(a)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_true=evaluate_sequence(list_from_array(a))
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   eval_count=0; t_start...
   while eval_count<max_evals and time<...:
       # compute gradient direction using LSE high beta or active peaks
       c=metric_conv(a,kind); y=c[S]; M=...
       # choose active top m= min(20,len(S)); weights softmax with beta=100/c_scale
       p_S = softmax_raw(y? maybe top)
       gL = grad_weights raw; grad_u = a*(gL*scale - dot)
       if norm>0: direction = -grad_u
       # try a small set t: scale=1/||direction||? Use unit
       d=direction/(norm+1e-30)
       # t list [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0]
       improved=False
       for t in ...:
           a_c = softmax_u(u + t*d)
           val=eval_true; count++
           if val<best_true: best...; break
       if not improved:
           # random smooth perturbation direction
           f = rng.integers(1,6)
           ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
           sigma = 0.01
           a_p = a*(1+sigma*np.cos(2π f * arange(n)/n + ph))
           a_p = normalize_nonneg(a_p)
           val=eval; count++; if improve: best=a_p; u=log(a_p)
       # occasionally random Fourier multi-sine
   return best_a,best_true
```

Linear LSE:
```
def linear_lse(a,S,beta):
 c=linear_conv(a)
 S=np.asarray(S); y=c[S]
 cmax=float(np.max(y))
 if cmax<=0: return 0, p uniform?
 shifted=beta*(y-cmax)
 # for stability if max shifted 0, min -inf
 exp=np.exp(shifted) # if shifted has nan? sanitize
 exp_sum=exp.sum()
 if exp_sum<=0: p=np.full(y.size,1/y.size); L=cmax
 else: p=exp/exp_sum; L=cmax + log(exp_sum)/beta
 return L,p
```
If beta huge and y-cmax has -inf? y finite; shifted may be -1e308? If y diff -1 and beta 1e308? We cap betas ~3e6, diff ~1, shifted -3e6; exp 0. Good.

`linear_grad_weights`:
```
def linear_grad_weights(a,p_S,S):
 p_S=np.asarray(p_S,float); S=np.asarray(S,int)
 nz = p_S > 1e-300
 if not nz.any(): return zeros
 n=a.size
 if nz.sum() <= max(16, n//64): direct
 else:
   L=2*n-1
   p_full=np.zeros(L); p_full[S[nz]]=p_S[nz] # p_S zero elsewhere
   a_rev=a[::-1]
   M=len(p_full)+n-1 #3n-2
   size=1<<max(0, M-1).bit_length(); if size<M: size<<=1
   P=np.fft.rfft(p_full,size); AR=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,size)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(P*AR,size)
   g=2*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check slice: conv length size, but data length M=3n-2. Need length n from index n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive (2n-1). `conv[n-1:2*n-1]` gives indices n-1...2n-2, length n. Good.
Direct:
```
g=np.zeros(n)
for j,w in zip(S[nz],p_S[nz]):
   j=int(j)
   js=max(0,j-(n-1)); je=min(j,n-1)
   if je>=js:
      m0=j-je
      m1=j-js
      g[js:je+1] += 2.0*w*a[m0:m1+1][::-1]
return g
```
Example n=5,j=6: js=2,je=4; m0=2,m1=4; i=2 m=4, i3 m=3, i4 m=2; a[2:5][::-1] = [a4,a3,a2]. Good.

def lse_u_objective(u, beta, kind, S, ent_reg, scale_obj):
    a=softmax_u(u)
    if kind[0]=='l':
       L,p=linear_lse(a,S,beta)
       gL=linear_grad_weights(a,p,S)
    else:
       L,p=circular_lse(a,S,beta)
       gL=circular_grad_weights(a,p,S)
    L=float(L)*scale_obj
    gL=gL*scale_obj
    if ent_reg != 0.0:
       loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
       L -= ent_reg*np.sum(a*loga)
       gL = gL - ent_reg*(loga+1.0)
    gd=np.dot(a,gL)
    grad=a*(gL-gd)
    if not finite: ...
    return val, grad
```
Note: If `scale_obj` is 1/core, raw L ~core, L_scaled ~1. Ent `-ent*H` where H negative. If ent 1e-3, L changes ~ -ent*H ~ +ent log n? Actually H=sum a log a negative; -ent*H positive. Minimizing will reduce it (increase entropy). okay.

def adam_optimize_u(u0, is_linear,S,beta,scale_obj,lr=0.05,max_iters=2000,max_time=60.0,ent_reg=0.0):
    u=u0.copy(); best_a=...; best_core=...; m=zeros; v=zeros; t0=time
    for it:
       val,grad = lse_u_objective(u,beta,is_linear,S,ent_reg,scale_obj)
       if not finite: break
       m=0.9*m+0.1*grad; v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
       u -= lr * m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
       u -= np.mean(u)
       u = np.clip(u,-100,100)
       a=softmax_u(u); core=metric_core(a,is_linear,S)

       if time > max_time or it%50==0 and core... maybe continue
    return best_a,best_core,u

```
def build_initial_arrays(n,parent,rng,max_inits=4):
    out=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size>1:
       p=normalize_nonneg(parent)
       if p.size==n: out.append(p.copy())
       else: out.append(resample_parent(p,n))
    # uniform
    out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    # edge enhanced
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(0.01+0.9*np.abs(2*x-1),1e-6)))
    # center cosine high
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(0.01+0.9*(0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)),1e-6)))
    # two endpoint Gaussians
    if n>2:
       d0=np.minimum(x,1-x) # distance to nearest endpoint? high endpoints
       sig = np.exp(-8.0 * (x*4)?)
```
Use endpoint bumps: `bump = np.exp(-40.0*x*x) + np.exp(-40.0*(1-x)*(1-x))`; normalize.
    # random smooth
    sig = np.ones(n)*0.5
    for f in range(1,5):
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.15)
       sig += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(sig,1e-4)))
    # random low noise
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(0.1+0.4*rng.random(n)))

Now, `make_betas(n, core)`:

def make_betas(n, core):
    c = max(float(core), 1.0/max(int(n),1))
    base = 1.0/c
    vals = [0.5,2,6,20,60,200]
    betas=[]
    for v in vals:
       b=int(round(v*base))
       if b>0: betas.append(b)
    # also add 10*n? base maybe similar; de-dup sorted
    # cap maybe 1e8
    betas=sorted(set(min(b,10**8) for b in betas if b>0))
    if not betas: betas=[n]
    return betas
```

       beta = 10*scale? Since scale_obj=1/core, beta=10*core? Wait raw core ~1/scale. We can compute core=metric_core(a); beta=10/max(core,1e-12).

def softmax_u(u):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
      u=np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   m=np.max(u)
   z=np.exp(u-m)
   s=z.sum()
   if s<1e-300 or not finite: return ones/n
   return z/s
```

`fft_size_lin(n)`:
```
def fft_size_lin(n):
    M=2*int(n)-1
    if M <=1: return 1
    size = 1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    if size < M: size <<=1
    return size
```
For M=1, (0).bit_length=0 ->1. okay.
`fft_size_grad(x_len,p_len)` maybe:
```
def fft_size_conv(n,p_len):
   M=n+p_len-1
   size=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
   if size < M: size <<=1
   return size
```
For p_len=L.

`linear_grad_weights(a,p_S,S)` with p_S length y. If p_S empty return zeros.
```
p_S=np.asarray(p_S,float); S=np.asarray(S,int)
nz=p_S>1e-300
if not nz.any(): return zeros
n=a.size
nz_idx=np.flatnonzero(nz)
if nz_idx.size > max(16, n//64):
   L=2*n-1
   p_full=np.zeros(L,float)
   np.add.at(p_full, S[nz], p_S[nz])
   a_rev=a[::-1]
   M=L+n-1
   size=1 << (M-1).bit_length(); if size<M: size<<=1
   P=np.fft.rfft(p_full,size); AR=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,size)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(P*AR,size)
   g=2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
else:
   g=np.zeros(n,float)
   for idx in nz_idx:
      j=int(S[idx]); w=float(p_S[idx])
      js=max(0,j-(n-1)); je=min(j,n-1)
      if je>=js:
         m0=j-je; m1=j-js
         g[js:je+1] += 2.0*w*a[m0:m1+1][::-1]
return g
```
If S contains out-of-range? make_indices ensures. `np.add.at` maybe p_full length L; if S duplicate, sums. Good.
Direct loop: if many NZ but `n//64` small, use FFT. For n=8, threshold max16, direct if <=16 (likely all). Good.

`circular_grad_weights`:
```

if nz_idx.size > max(16, n//64):
   p_full=np.zeros(n)
   S_mod = np.mod(S[nz], n)
   np.add.at(p_full, S_mod, p_S[nz])

else:
   g=zeros
   for idx:
     k=int(S[idx])%n; w=p_S[idx]
     if k==0: g += 2*w*a
     else: g += w*(np.roll(a,k)+np.roll(a,-k))
   # Wait if p includes both k and n-k, direct does both; if p includes multiple, sum.
return g
```

```
def run_lbfgs_metric(u0,is_linear,S,max_total_sec,betas,ent_reg=1e-4,maxfun_per_beta=2000):
   t_start=time.monotonic(); u=np.asarray(u0,float).copy(); a=softmax_u(u); core=metric_core(a,is_linear,S); scale=1.0/max(core,1e-12); best_a=a.copy(); best_core=core
   n=u.size
   fun_rate = max(10.0, min(400.0, 2000000.0/float(max(n,1))))
   for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
      # ensure beta finite positive
      beta=float(beta)
      if beta<=0: continue
      ent = ent_reg if idx < len(betas)//2 else 0.0
      rem = max_total_sec - (time.monotonic()-t_start)
      if rem < 20: break
      maxfun = int(min(maxfun_per_beta, max(200, rem*fun_rate)))
      maxiter = int(min(2000, max(100, maxfun//5)))
      try:
         res = minimize(lse_u_objective, u, args=(beta,is_linear,S,ent,scale), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11,'maxls':50,'disp':0})
         if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            u = np.asarray(res.x,float)
      except Exception:
         pass
      a=softmax_u(u); core=metric_core(a,is_linear,S)
      if core < best_core:
         best_core=core; best_a=a.copy()
      # if core very small? not.
   return best_a,best_core,u
```
Could set `gtol=1e-12`. Since gradient scale maybe 1/n, gtol 1e-11 okay. Use 1e-12? More iterations. Use 1e-10? For high precision, 1e-12. We'll use `1e-12` and maxfun capped.

`adam_optimize_u`:

def adam_optimize_u(u0,is_linear,S,beta,scale_obj,lr=0.05,max_iters=2000,max_time=60.0,ent_reg=0.0):
   t_start=time.monotonic(); u0=np.asarray(u0,float).copy(); a=softmax_u(u0); best_core=metric_core(a,is_linear,S); best_a=a.copy(); best_u=u0.copy()
   m=np.zeros_like(u); v=np.zeros_like(u)
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()-t_start > max_time: break
      try: val,grad = lse_u_objective(u,float(beta),is_linear,S,float(ent_reg),float(scale_obj))
      except: break
      if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
      m=0.9*m+0.1*grad; v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
      u = u - lr*m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
      u = u - float(np.mean(u))
      u = np.clip(u,-100.0,100.0)
      a=softmax_u(u); core=metric_core(a,is_linear,S)
      if core<best_core:
         best_core=core; best_a=a.copy(); best_u=u.copy()
      # if core no improvement for 200 iters? maybe break? no.
   return best_a,best_core,best_u
```
Use `beta` maybe raw high. `scale_obj` raw core inverse. If `beta` huge and scaled objective, okay.

def fienup_refine(a0,is_linear,S,n_iters=100,alpha_start=0.1,alpha_end=0.8,max_time=30.0):

`soft_nonneg`:
```
def soft_nonneg(x,slope=30.0):
   z=np.clip(x,-50.0,50.0)
   gate=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-slope*z))
   y=x*gate
   return np.maximum(y,0.0)
```
For x large positive, gate ~1; for negative, small. Scaling may reduce sum; normalize after.

Now, `true_line_search` top-level. Need `core` for beta. It can use `metric_core` and `make_betas`? Use fixed beta = 20*scale_obj? If scale_obj=1/core, beta=20/core. Good.
```
def true_line_search(a0,is_linear,S,scale_obj,max_evals,max_time,seed=999):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a0); u=log_array(a); n=a.size
   best_a=a.copy(); best_true=evaluate_safe(a)
   if not finite: return best_a,np.inf
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   t_start=time.monotonic(); evals=0; step=0.05
   max_dirs = max(1, int(max_evals*1.5))
   for it in range(max_dirs):
      if evals>=max_evals or time>max_time: break
      core=metric_core(a,is_linear,S)
      if core<=0: break
      beta=20.0/max(core,1e-12)
      if is_linear:
         _,p=linear_lse(a,S,beta)
         gL=linear_grad_weights(a,p,S)
      else:
         _,p=circular_lse(a,S,beta)
         gL=circular_grad_weights(a,p,S)
      gd=float(np.dot(a,gL)); gu=a*(gL-gd)
      gn=float(np.linalg.norm(gu))
      if gn<1e-18 or not finite:
         # random only
         if evals<max_evals and time<...:
             a_c=random_smooth_perturb(a,rng); val=eval_safe; evals++; update
         step=0.05; continue
      d=-gu/gn
      improved=False
      for trial in range(3):
         if evals>=max_evals or time>max_time: break
         a_c=softmax_u(u + step*d)
         val=evaluate_safe(a_c); evals+=1
         if math.isfinite(val) and val<best_true:
            best_true=val; best_a=a_c.copy(); u=log_array(best_a); step=min(0.5, step*1.25); improved=True; break
         step*=0.5
      if not improved:
         # every 3 iterations random smooth
         if it % 3 == 0 and evals<max_evals:
            a_c=random_smooth_perturb(a,rng,amp=0.02)
            val=evaluate_safe(a_c); evals+=1
            if val<best_true: update
         step=0.03
   return best_a,best_true
```
Need `random_smooth_perturb` top-level:
```
def random_smooth_perturb(a,rng,amp=None):
   n=a.size
   if amp is None: amp=0.01+0.02*rng.random()
   f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//2)))
   ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   x=np.arange(n)
   noise=amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x/float(n)+ph)
   # add second harmonic smaller
   f2=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//2)))
   noise += 0.5*amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f2*x/float(n)+rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   c=a+noise
   return normalize_nonneg(c)
```
Need if f > n? use min.

`max_evals` estimate. In propose:
```

SoTA: Given a (sum normalized), M=core(a). Solve approximately LP max sum g s.t. c(f*g)_j <= M for j∈S, g≥0. We can approximate via **projected gradient on g** with soft constraints and normalize. Since objective linear, constraints linear. We can solve primal dual using **Bregman**:
Maximize ∑g - τ∑g log g (concave) s.t. A g <= M, g≥0. Entropy barrier makes strictly concave. Primal mirror descent: g_{t+1} = g_t * exp(-η_t (1 - A^T λ_t))? From KKT. Dual update λ += η(Ag-M)^+. Could implement:
- Maintain g positive sum1, λ≥0.
- For iterations:
  * Compute h = A^T λ = weighted conv of λ and a (linear/circ).
  * g ← g * exp(-μ (1 - h)) / sum; then maybe project to satisfy Ag <= M by scaling? To enforce Ag<=M, use penalty: g ← g * exp(-η ( (1-h) + α*(Ag-M)_+? )?
  * Dual λ ← λ + γ max(0, Ag - M)/M.
This is alternation. Might be useful. But implementation complex and may not outperform LSE.

Another SoTA simplification: For fixed a, the LP optimum g can be found by **active-set**: dual constraints h_i ≥1; choose λ to minimize M∑λ. Since M current core, dual objective scale. We can solve dual via **entropy-regularized regression**: minimize `M∑λ + ε∑λlogλ + (ρ/2)∑max(0,1-h_i)^2`. This is convex in λ (A^T λ affine; max square convex; entropy convex). Gradient through FFT. Then given λ, set g_i ∝ exp(h_i?)? For entropy primal, KKT: 1 + ε log g_i - μ - (A^Tλ)_i =0? Since max ∑g - ε∑glogg. Actually dual of entropy program leads g_i = exp((μ + h_i -1)/ε). To maximize sum, choose μ such that? This yields a smooth g. Could use as update. Maybe not.

```
def metric_score_scaled_kind(a, kind):
    n=a.size
    if n<=0: return np.inf
    is_lin=kind.startswith('lin')
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s<=1e-300: return np.inf
    M=2.0*n
    S=make_indices(kind,n)
    c=metric_conv(a,is_lin)
    if c.size==0 or S.size==0: return np.inf
    v=float(np.max(np.maximum(c[S],0.0)))
    return M*v/(s*s)
```

`choose_lengths(parent, budget)`:
```
def choose_lengths(parent,budget):
    p_len = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 0
    if 500 <= p_len <= 100000: base=p_len
    else: base=8192
    max_len_count = 4 if budget>=1800 else (3 if budget>=1200 else 2)
    powers=[4096,8192,12288,16384,24576,32768]
    lens=[base]
    for p in powers:
       if p not in lens:
          if 0.45*base <= p <= 2.2*base:
             if len(lens)<max_len_count: lens.append(p)
    # ensure if base <8192 and 8192 not in lens, add if room
    if base <8192 and 8192 not in lens and len(lens)<max_len_count: lens.append(8192)
    # ensure larger if budget enough
    if len(lens)<max_len_count:
       bigger = next((p for p in powers if p > max(lens) and p <= 32768), None)
       if bigger: lens.append(bigger)
    lens=sorted(set(lens))
    return lens
```
If base 6962, criterion 3132..15316 adds 8192,12288,16384? 16384 >15316 no. But we want maybe 16384; criterion <=2.2*base (15316) excludes 16384. Set 2.5. Add 2.5. Or add all powers up to 2*base rounded. Use ratio 2.5. For base6962, 16384 ratio2.35 add if <=2.5. Good. Max count 4: lens [6962,8192,12288,16384]. Sort. If budget 2400 max4. Good.
If base 2000, criterion add 4096,8192? 8192 ratio4.1 no, then ensure bigger 8192? If len<max, add next >max maybe4096, but 8192 needed. We can explicitly add 8192 if base<8192. Then maybe 16384 if len. Good.

Implementation no nested:
```
def build_initial_arrays(n,parent,rng,max_inits=4):
    out=[]
    if max_inits <=0: max_inits=1
    if parent is not None and parent.size>1:
        p=normalize_nonneg(parent)
        if p.size==n: out.append(p.copy())
        else: out.append(resample_parent(p,n))
    out.append(np.ones(n)/float(n))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(0.01 + 0.9*np.abs(2.0*x-1.0), 1e-8)))
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(0.01 + 0.9*(0.5+0.5*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)), 1e-8)))
    if n > 2:
       end_bump = np.exp(-30.0*x*x) + np.exp(-30.0*(1.0-x)*(1.0-x))
       out.append(normalize_nonneg(end_bump + 1e-4))
    sig=np.ones(n)*0.5
    for f in range(1,5):
       sig = sig + 0.08*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(sig,1e-4)))
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(0.1 + 0.3*rng.random(n)))
    if len(out)>max_inits: out=out[:max_inits]
    return out
```
For n huge, `rng.random(n)` memory okay. If parent resample plus uniform repeated maybe; deduplicate not necessary. If max_inits=1 returns parent if available, else uniform? We put parent first; if not, uniform second, but if max_inits=1 and no parent, out[:1] returns uniform. Good.

Pseudo final propose with local variables (not nested):
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=2400.0):
    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=float(budget_s); deadline=t0+budget-45.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # quick eval probe
    probe_n=256
    probe=np.ones(probe_n)/probe_n
    tp=time.monotonic(); probe_val=evaluate_safe(probe); t_probe=time.monotonic()-tp
    # calibrate
    kinds=calibrate_metric(3)
    print...
    parent=get_parent_array()
    # initial best
    if parent is not None and 512 <= parent.size <= 100000:
        best_a=normalize_nonneg(parent)
    elif parent is not None and parent.size>1:
        best_a=normalize_nonneg(resample_parent(parent,8192))
    else:
        best_a=np.ones(8192)/8192
    best_true=evaluate_safe(best_a) if len(best_a)<=100000 else np.inf
    best_kind = kinds[0] if kinds else 'lin_noends'
    # constant candidate baseline
    const_a = np.ones(len(best_a))/len(best_a)
    valc=evaluate_safe(const_a)
    if valc<best_true: best_true=valc; best_a=const_a; best_kind=kinds[0]
    # early full
    if len(kinds)>0 and kinds[0]=='lin_full' and 1.999 <= best_true <= 2.001:
        out=list_from_array(best_a); val=evaluate_safe(out); print...; return out
    # lengths
    lengths=choose_lengths(parent,budget)
    print lengths
    # for each length
    for li,N in enumerate(lengths):
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 180.0: break
        remaining=deadline-now
        if li==0: length_budget=min(1100.0, remaining*0.50)
        elif li==1: length_budget=min(700.0, remaining*0.25)
        elif li==2: length_budget=min(450.0, remaining*0.20)
        else: length_budget=min(250.0, remaining*0.15)
        if length_budget < 90.0: break
        max_inits = 4 if li==0 else 3
        inits=build_initial_arrays(N,parent,rng,max_inits)
        # choose metric kinds to optimize for this length
        if length_budget > 500.0 and len(kinds)>=2: metric_kinds=kinds[:2]
        else: metric_kinds=[kinds[0]]
        for ki,kind in enumerate(metric_kinds):
            now=time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline - 180.0: break
            is_lin = kind.startswith('lin')
            S = make_indices(kind,N)
            if S.size==0: continue
            # number of inits for this kind
            n_inits = len(inits) if ki==0 else min(2,len(inits))
            # per-run budget
            kind_run_budget = length_budget / float(len(metric_kinds))
            for ai in range(n_inits):
                now=time.monotonic()
                if now > deadline - 180.0: break
                a0 = inits[ai]
                u0 = log_array(a0)
                core0 = metric_core(a0,is_lin,S)
                scale0 = min(1.0/max(float(core0),1e-12), 1e6)
                betas = make_betas(N,float(core0))
                run_budget = min(220.0, max(45.0, kind_run_budget * (1.0 / max(1.0, float(len(inits))))))
                # maybe further cap by remaining
                run_budget = min(run_budget, deadline - time.monotonic() - 180.0)
                if run_budget < 30: break
                a_lb,core_lb,u_lb = run_lbfgs_metric(u0,is_lin,S,run_budget,betas,ent_reg=1e-4,maxfun_per_beta=2000)
                # fienup
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
                    a_fi,core_fi = fienup_refine(a_lb,is_lin,S,n_iters=60,max_time=min(30.0,run_budget*0.15))
                else: a_fi=a_lb; core_fi=core_lb
                if core_fi < core_lb:
                    a_cur=a_fi; core_cur=core_fi; u_cur=log_array(a_fi)
                else:
                    a_cur=a_lb; core_cur=core_lb; u_cur=u_lb
                # Adam
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0 and len(betas)>0:
                    beta_adam = betas[-1]
                    a_ad,core_ad,u_ad = adam_optimize_u(u_cur,is_lin,S,beta_ad,scale0,lr=0.03,max_time=min(30.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.05))
                    if core_ad < core_cur:
                        a_cur=a_ad; core_cur=core_ad; u_cur=u_ad
                # true eval
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 and N <= 100000:
                    val = evaluate_safe(a_cur)
                    if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_true:
                        best_true = val
                        best_a = a_cur.copy()
                        best_kind = kind
                        print update
                    else:
                        # don't print every
                        pass
                # keep local best by core? not needed.
    # final constant check for best length maybe
    const_a = np.ones(len(best_a))/len(best_a)
    valc=evaluate_safe(const_a)
    if valc < best_true:
       best_true=valc; best_a=const_a; best_kind=kinds[0]
    # final line search
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        N=len(best_a)
        if 1 <= N <= 100000:
            S=make_indices(best_kind,N)
            is_lin=best_kind.startswith('lin')
            core=metric_core(best_a,is_lin,S)
            scale=min(1/max(core,1e-12),1e6)
            # max evals based on time and probe
            max_time = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
            if t_probe > 1e-6:
                est = t_probe * (float(N)/256.0)**2
            else:
                est = 0.001 + 0.0000005*float(N)**2/1e6
            max_evals = int(max(20, min(120, max_time/(est*3.0+0.01))))
            a_final, true_final = true_line_search(best_a,is_lin,S,scale,max_evals,max_time,seed=seed+17)
            if math.isfinite(true_final) and true_final < best_true:
                best_true=true_final; best_a=a_final; print
    # final verify
    out=list_from_array(best_a)
    final_val=evaluate_safe(out)
    if not math.isfinite(final_val):
       # fallback parent
       if parent is not None and parent.size>64:
           out=list_from_array(normalize_nonneg(parent)); final_val=evaluate_safe(out)
       if not math.isfinite(final_val):
           out=np.ones(1024); out=out/1024.0; final_val=evaluate_safe(out)
    print final
    return out
```

```
def make_betas(n,core):
    if n <1: n=1
    c=max(float(core),1.0/float(n),1e-18)
    base=1.0/c
    raw=[0.5,2,5,20,60,200]
    betas=[]
    for v in raw:
       b=int(round(v*base))
       if b>0: betas.append(min(b,10**8))
    if not betas: betas=[int(n)]
    return sorted(set(betas))
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing the supplied
evaluation function.  The program first calibrates a small set of plausible
convolution-index interpretations against the external evaluate_sequence()
oracle, then optimizes the calibrated objective using softmax-parameterized
log-sum-exp / L-BFGS, spectral Fienup shaping, Adam polishing, and a final
black-box line search using the true evaluator.  Multiple lengths and metric
candidates are tried; the best true score is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def softmax_u(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        u = np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = float(np.max(u))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        m = 0.0
    z = np.exp(u - m)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(u.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, u.size))
    return z / s


def log_array(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        if a.size > 0:
            return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def list_from_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        if a.size > 0:
            a = np.full(a.size, 0.1 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a = np.array([0.1], dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(list_from_array(a))
        val = float(val)
        if math.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float("inf")


def get_parent_array():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0:
            return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def resample_parent(p, n):
    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if p.size == n:
        return p.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, p.size, n, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(xs, np.arange(p.size, dtype=np.float64), p)
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(out, 0.0))


def build_initial_arrays(n, parent, rng, max_inits=4):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    out = []
    if max_inits <= 0:
        max_inits = 1

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 1:
        p = normalize_nonneg(parent)
        if p.size == n:
            out.append(p.copy())
        else:
            out.append(resample_parent(p, n))

    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(0.01 + 0.9 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0), 1e-8)))
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(0.01 + 0.9 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)), 1e-8)))

    if n > 2:
        end_bump = np.exp(-30.0 * x * x) + np.exp(-30.0 * (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x))
        out.append(normalize_nonneg(end_bump + 1e-4))

    sig = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    for f in range(1, 5):
        sig = sig + 0.08 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    out.append(normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(sig, 1e-4)))

    out.append(normalize_nonneg(0.1 + 0.3 * rng.random(n)))

    if len(out) > max_inits:
        out = out[:max_inits]
    return out


def fft_size_lin(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    size = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    if size < m:
        size <<= 1
    return size


def linear_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = fft_size_lin(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[: 2 * n - 1]


def circular_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.fft(a)
    return np.fft.ifft(A * A).real


def make_indices(kind, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    full = 2 * n - 1

    if kind == "lin_full":
        return np.arange(full, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_no0":
        return np.arange(1, full, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_noends":
        return np.arange(1, full - 1, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_same":
        start = (n - 1) // 2
        return np.arange(start, start + n, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_first":
        return np.arange(0, n, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_second":
        return np.arange(n - 1, full, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_tail":
        return np.arange(n, full, dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_first0":
        if n > 1:
            return np.arange(0, n - 1, dtype=int)
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_last0":
        if n > 1:
            return np.arange(n, full, dtype=int)
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if kind == "lin_center":
        start = max(0, (2 * n - 2) - (n // 2))
        end = min(full, start + n)
        return np.arange(start, end, dtype=int)
    if kind == "circ_full":
        return np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    if kind == "circ_no0":
        return np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
    if kind == "circ_half":
        return np.arange(0, n // 2 + 1, dtype=int)

    return np.arange(full, dtype=int)


def metric_conv(a, is_linear):
    if is_linear:
        return linear_conv(a)
    return circular_conv(a)


def metric_core(a, is_linear, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=int)
    if S.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = metric_conv(a, is_linear)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.maximum(c[S], 0.0)))


def metric_score_scaled_kind(a, kind):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    is_lin = kind.startswith("lin")
    S = make_indices(kind, n)
    if S.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = metric_conv(a, is_lin)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(np.maximum(c[S], 0.0)))
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def linear_lse(a, S, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=int)
    if S.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = linear_conv(a)
    y = np.asarray(c[S], dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(y))
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return max(cmax, 0.0), p
    beta = float(max(1e-12, beta))
    shifted = beta * (y - cmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return cmax, p
    p = ex / s
    L = cmax + math.log(s) / beta
    return float(L), p


def linear_grad_weights(a, p_S, S):
    n = int(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).size)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p_S = np.asarray(p_S, dtype=np.float64)
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=int)

    if S.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    nz = p_S > 1e-300
    if not np.any(nz):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    nz_idx = np.flatnonzero(nz)
    if nz_idx.size > max(16, n // 64):
        L = 2 * n - 1
        p_full = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        np.add.at(p_full, S[nz], p_S[nz])
        a_rev = a[::-1]
        M = L + n - 1
        size = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
        if size < M:
            size <<= 1
        P = np.fft.rfft(p_full, size)
        AR = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, size)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * AR, size)
        g = 2.0 * conv[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1]
        return np.asarray(g[:n], dtype=np.float64)

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx in nz_idx:
        j = int(S[idx])
        w = float(p_S[idx])
        js = max(0, j - (n - 1))
        je = min(j, n - 1)
        if je >= js:
            m0 = j - je
            m1 = j - js
            g[js : je + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[m0 : m1 + 1][::-1]
    return g


def circular_lse(a, S, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=int)
    if S.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = circular_conv(a)
    y = np.asarray(np.maximum(c[S], 0.0), dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(y))
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return max(cmax, 0.0), p
    beta = float(max(1e-12, beta))
    shifted = beta * (y - cmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return cmax, p
    p = ex / s
    L = cmax + math.log(s) / beta
    return float(L), p


def circular_grad_weights(a, p_S, S):
    n = int(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).size)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p_S = np.asarray(p_S, dtype=np.float64)
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=int)

    if S.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    nz = p_S > 1e-300
    if not np.any(nz):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    nz_idx = np.flatnonzero(nz)
    if nz_idx.size > max(16, n // 64):
        p_full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        S_mod = np.mod(S[nz], n).astype(int)
        np.add.at(p_full, S_mod, p_S[nz])
        A = np.fft.fft(a)
        P = np.fft.fft(p_full)
        g = np.fft.ifft((P + np.conj(P)) * A).real
        return np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx in nz_idx:
        k = int(S[idx]) % n
        w = float(p_S[idx])
        if k == 0:
            g += 2.0 * w * a
        else:
            g += w * (np.roll(a, k) + np.roll(a, -k))
    return g


def lse_u_objective(u, beta, is_linear, S, ent_reg, scale_obj):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    a = softmax_u(u)

    if is_linear:
        L, p = linear_lse(a, S, beta)
        gL = linear_grad_weights(a, p, S)
    else:
        L, p = circular_lse(a, S, beta)
        gL = circular_grad_weights(a, p, S)

    scale = float(max(1e-300, scale_obj))
    L = float(L) * scale
    gL = np.asarray(gL, dtype=np.float64) * scale

    if ent_reg != 0.0:
        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        L = L - float(ent_reg) * float(np.sum(a * loga))
        gL = gL - float(ent_reg) * (loga + 1.0)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, gL))
    grad = a * (gL - dot)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(grad)
    if not math.isfinite(L):
        L = 1e100
    return float(L), grad


def make_betas(n, core):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    c = max(float(core), 1.0 / float(n), 1e-18)
    base = 1.0 / c
    raw = [0.5, 2.0, 5.0, 20.0, 60.0, 200.0]
    out = []
    for v in raw:
        b = int(round(v * base))
        if b > 0:
            out.append(min(b, 100000000))
    out = sorted(set(out))
    if not out:
        out = [max(1, int(n))]
    return out


def run_lbfgs_metric(u0, is_linear, S, max_total_sec, betas, ent_reg=1e-4, maxfun_per_beta=2000):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    u = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = softmax_u(u)
    core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
    scale = min(1.0 / max(float(core), 1e-12), 1.0e6)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_core = float(core)
    n = int(u.size)
    fun_rate = max(10.0, min(400.0, 2000000.0 / float(max(1, n))))

    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        beta = float(max(1.0, beta))
        ent = float(ent_reg) if idx < max(1, len(betas) // 2) else 0.0
        rem = float(max_total_sec) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(min(maxfun_per_beta, max(200, int(rem * fun_rate))))
        maxiter = int(min(2000, max(100, maxfun // 5)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_u_objective,
                u,
                args=(beta, is_linear, S, ent, scale),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "disp": 0,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass

        a = softmax_u(u)
        core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
        if core < best_core:
            best_core = float(core)
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_core, u


def adam_optimize_u(u0, is_linear, S, beta, scale_obj, lr=0.05, max_iters=2000, max_time=60.0, ent_reg=0.0):
    if max_time <= 1.0:
        a = softmax_u(u0)
        return a, metric_core(a, is_linear, S), np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    u = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = softmax_u(u)
    best_core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_u = u.copy()

    m = np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(max(1.0, beta))
    scale = float(max(1e-300, scale_obj))

    for _ in range(max(0, int(max_iters))):
        if time.monotonic() - t_start > float(max_time):
            break
        try:
            _, grad = lse_u_objective(u, beta, is_linear, S, float(ent_reg), scale)
        except Exception:
            break
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break
        m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * grad
        v = 0.999 * v + 0.001 * (grad * grad)
        u = u - float(lr) * m / (np.sqrt(v) + 1e-8)
        u = u - float(np.mean(u))
        u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 100.0)
        a = softmax_u(u)
        core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
        if core < best_core:
            best_core = float(core)
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_u = u.copy()

    return best_a, best_core, best_u


def soft_nonneg(x, slope=30.0):
    z = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
    gate = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-float(slope) * z))
    y = x * gate
    return np.maximum(y, 0.0)


def fienup_refine(a0, is_linear, S, n_iters=100, alpha_start=0.1, alpha_end=0.8, max_time=30.0):
    if max_time <= 1.0:
        return normalize_nonneg(a0), metric_core(normalize_nonneg(a0), is_linear, S)

    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    if is_linear:
        size = fft_size_lin(n)

    for it in range(max(0, int(n_iters))):
        if time.monotonic() - t_start > float(max_time):
            break
        alpha = float(alpha_start) + (float(alpha_end) - float(alpha_start)) * (
            float(it) / float(max(1, int(n_iters)))
        )

        if is_linear:
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
            amp = np.abs(A)
            phase = np.angle(A)
            if amp.size > 1:
                C = float(np.median(amp[1:]))
            else:
                C = float(amp[0])
            C = max(C, 1e-300)
            amp_safe = np.maximum(amp, 1e-300)
            amp_new = amp_safe * (C / amp_safe) ** alpha
            a_new = np.fft.irfft(amp_new * np.exp(1j * phase), size)[:n]
        else:
            A = np.fft.fft(a)
            amp = np.abs(A)
            phase = np.angle(A)
            if amp.size > 1:
                C = float(np.median(amp[1:]))
            else:
                C = float(amp[0])
            C = max(C, 1e-300)
            amp_safe = np.maximum(amp, 1e-300)
            amp_new = amp_safe * (C / amp_safe) ** alpha
            a_new = np.fft.ifft(amp_new * np.exp(1j * phase)).real

        a_new = soft_nonneg(a_new, slope=30.0)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        s = float(a_new.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
            break
        a = a_new / s
        core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
        if core < best_core:
            best_core = float(core)
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_core


def random_smooth_perturb(a, rng, amp=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if amp is None:
        amp = 0.01 + 0.02 * float(rng.random())
    maxf = max(2, min(n // 2, 1000))
    f1 = int(rng.integers(1, maxf + 1))
    f2 = int(rng.integers(1, maxf + 1))
    ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x / float(n) + ph1)
    noise += 0.5 * amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x / float(n) + ph2)
    return normalize_nonneg(a + noise)


def true_line_search(a0, is_linear, S, scale_obj, max_evals, max_time, seed=1234):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    u = log_array(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_true = evaluate_safe(a)
    if max_evals < 1 or max_time < 10.0 or not math.isfinite(best_true):
        return best_a, best_true

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    evals = 0
    step = 0.05
    n = int(a.size)
    max_dirs = max(1, int(max_evals * 1.5))

    for it in range(max_dirs):
        if evals >= int(max_evals) or time.monotonic() - t_start > float(max_time):
            break

        core = metric_core(a, is_linear, S)
        if core <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(core):
            break
        beta = 20.0 / max(core, 1e-12)

        if is_linear:
            _, p = linear_lse(a, S, beta)
            gL = linear_grad_weights(a, p, S)
        else:
            _, p = circular_lse(a, S, beta)
            gL = circular_grad_weights(a, p, S)

        dot = float(np.dot(a, gL))
        gu = a * (gL - dot)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(gu))

        if not math.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-18:
            if evals < int(max_evals) and time.monotonic() - t_start < float(max_time):
                cand = random_smooth_perturb(a, rng)
                val = evaluate_safe(cand)
                evals += 1
                if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_true:
                    best_true = val
                    best_a = cand.copy()
            step = 0.05
            continue

        d = -gu / gn
        improved = False
        for _ in range(3):
            if evals >= int(max_evals) or time.monotonic() - t_start > float(max_time):
                break
            cand = softmax_u(u + step * d)
            val = evaluate_safe(cand)
            evals += 1
            if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_true:
                best_true = val
                best_a = cand.copy()
                u = log_array(best_a)
                step = min(0.5, step * 1.25)
                improved = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            if it % 3 == 0 and evals < int(max_evals) and time.monotonic() - t_start < float(max_time):
                cand = random_smooth_perturb(a, rng, amp=0.02)
                val = evaluate_safe(cand)
                evals += 1
                if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_true:
                    best_true = val
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                    u = log_array(best_a)
            step = 0.03

    return best_a, best_true


def choose_lengths(parent, budget):
    p_len = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 0
    if 500 <= p_len <= 100000:
        base = p_len
    else:
        base = 8192

    budget = float(budget)
    max_count = 4 if budget >= 1800 else (3 if budget >= 1200 else 2)
    powers = [4096, 8192, 12288, 16384, 24576, 32768]

    lens = [base]
    for p in powers:
        if p not in lens and 0.45 * base <= p <= 2.5 * base:
            if len(lens) < max_count:
                lens.append(p)

    if base < 8192 and 8192 not in lens and len(lens) < max_count:
        lens.append(8192)

    if len(lens) < max_count:
        for p in powers:
            if p not in lens and p > max(lens):
                lens.append(p)
                break

    lens = sorted(set(int(x) for x in lens))
    return lens[:max_count]


def calibrate_metric(top_k=3):
    kinds = [
        "lin_full",
        "lin_no0",
        "lin_noends",
        "lin_same",
        "lin_first",
        "lin_second",
        "lin_tail",
        "lin_first0",
        "lin_last0",
        "lin_center",
        "circ_full",
        "circ_no0",
        "circ_half",
    ]
    ratios = {k: [] for k in kinds}
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240515)
    test_ns = [8, 10, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96]

    parent = get_parent_array()

    for n in test_ns:
        seqs = []
        seqs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

        edge = np.full(n, 0.05, dtype=np.float64)
        edge[0] = 2.0
        edge[-1] = 2.0
        seqs.append(edge)

        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
        seqs.append(np.maximum(0.2 + 0.8 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0), 0.01))
        seqs.append(np.maximum(0.2 + 0.8 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)), 0.01))

        sig = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
        for f in range(1, 5):
            sig += 0.08 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        seqs.append(np.maximum(sig, 0.01))

        seqs.append(np.maximum(0.1 + 0.4 * rng.random(n), 0.01))
        seqs.append(np.where((np.arange(n) % 2) == 0, 1.0, 0.05))
        seqs.append(np.linspace(0.5, 2.0, n))

        if parent is not None and parent.size >= n:
            seg = np.clip(parent[:n], 0.0, 1000.0).astype(np.float64)
            s = float(seg.sum())
            if math.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-6:
                seqs.append(seg * (5.0 / s))

        for seq in seqs:
            H = evaluate_safe(seq)
            if not math.isfinite(H):
                continue
            for kind in kinds:
                val = metric_score_scaled_kind(seq, kind)
                if math.isfinite(val) and val > 1e-300:
                    ratios[kind].append(H / val)

    results = []
    for kind in kinds:
        arr = np.asarray(ratios[kind], dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size < 15:
            continue
        logr = np.log(arr)
        std = float(np.std(logr))
        mean = float(np.mean(logr))
        cv = std / (abs(mean) + 1e-12)
        results.append((cv, abs(mean), kind))

    results.sort(key=lambda z: (z[0], z[1]))
    top = [z[2] for z in results[:top_k]]
    if not top:
        top = ["lin_noends", "lin_full", "circ_no0"]
    return top


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + budget - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    probe_n = 256
    probe = np.ones(probe_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(probe_n)
    tp = time.monotonic()
    evaluate_safe(probe)
    t_probe = time.monotonic() - tp

    kinds = calibrate_metric(3)
    print("Calibrated metric candidates:", kinds)

    parent = get_parent_array()

    if parent is not None and 512 <= parent.size <= 100000:
        best_a = normalize_nonneg(parent)
    elif parent is not None and parent.size > 1:
        best_a = normalize_nonneg(resample_parent(parent, 8192))
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0

    best_true = evaluate_safe(best_a) if best_a.size <= 100000 else float("inf")
    best_kind = kinds[0] if len(kinds) > 0 else "lin_noends"

    const_a = np.ones(best_a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(best_a.size)
    valc = evaluate_safe(const_a)
    if math.isfinite(valc) and valc < best_true:
        best_true = valc
        best_a = const_a.copy()

    print("Initial best true score:", best_true, "length:", best_a.size)

    if kinds and kinds[0] == "lin_full" and 1.999 <= best_true <= 2.001:
        out = list_from_array(best_a)
        final_val = evaluate_safe(out)
        print("Early outer bound (calibrated full metric):", final_val)
        return out

    lengths = choose_lengths(parent, budget)
    print("Trying lengths:", lengths)

    for li, N in enumerate(lengths):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 180.0:
            break
        remaining = deadline - now
        if li == 0:
            length_budget = min(1100.0, remaining * 0.50)
        elif li == 1:
            length_budget = min(700.0, remaining * 0.25)
        elif li == 2:
            length_budget = min(450.0, remaining * 0.20)
        else:
            length_budget = min(250.0, remaining * 0.15)

        if length_budget < 90.0:
            break

        max_inits = 4 if li == 0 else 3
        inits = build_initial_arrays(N, parent, rng, max_inits)

        if length_budget > 500.0 and len(kinds) >= 2:
            metric_kinds = kinds[:2]
        else:
            metric_kinds = [kinds[0]]

        for ki, kind in enumerate(metric_kinds):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline - 180.0:
                break

            is_lin = kind.startswith("lin")
            S = make_indices(kind, N)
            if S.size == 0:
                continue

            n_inits = len(inits) if ki == 0 else min(2, len(inits))
            kind_run_budget = length_budget / float(len(metric_kinds))

            for ai in range(n_inits):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now > deadline - 180.0:
                    break

                a0 = inits[ai]
                u0 = log_array(a0)
                core0 = metric_core(a0, is_lin, S)
                scale0 = min(1.0 / max(float(core0), 1e-12), 1.0e6)
                betas = make_betas(N, float(core0))

                run_budget = min(
                    220.0,
                    max(45.0, kind_run_budget / max(1.0, float(len(inits)))),
                    deadline - time.monotonic() - 180.0,
                )
                if run_budget < 30.0:
                    break

                a_lb, core_lb, u_lb = run_lbfgs_metric(
                    u0, is_lin, S, run_budget, betas, ent_reg=1e-4, maxfun_per_beta=2000
                )

                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
                    a_fi, core_fi = fienup_refine(
                        a_lb, is_lin, S, n_iters=60, max_time=min(30.0, run_budget * 0.15)
                    )
                else:
                    a_fi, core_fi = a_lb, core_lb

                if core_fi < core_lb:
                    a_cur, core_cur, u_cur = a_fi, core_fi, log_array(a_fi)
                else:
                    a_cur, core_cur, u_cur = a_lb, core_lb, u_lb

                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0 and len(betas) > 0:
                    beta_adam = betas[-1]
                    a_ad, core_ad, u_ad = adam_optimize_u(
                        u_cur,
                        is_lin,
                        S,
                        beta_adam,
                        scale0,
                        lr=0.03,
                        max_time=min(30.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.05),
                    )
                    if core_ad < core_cur:
                        a_cur, core_cur, u_cur = a_ad, core_ad, u_ad

                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 and N <= 100000:
                    val = evaluate_safe(a_cur)
                    if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_true:
                        best_true = val
                        best_a = np.asarray(a_cur, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        best_kind = kind
                        print(
                            f"Updated true best: {best_true:.9f}, N={N}, kind={kind}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s"
                        )

    const_a = np.ones(best_a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(best_a.size)
    valc = evaluate_safe(const_a)
    if math.isfinite(valc) and valc < best_true:
        best_true = valc
        best_a = const_a.copy()
        best_kind = kinds[0] if kinds else best_kind

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        N = int(best_a.size)
        if 1 <= N <= 100000 and best_kind is not None:
            S = make_indices(best_kind, N)
            is_lin = best_kind.startswith("lin")
            core = metric_core(best_a, is_lin, S)
            scale = min(1.0 / max(core, 1e-12), 1.0e6)
            max_time = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
            if t_probe > 1e-6:
                est = t_probe * (float(N) / 256.0) ** 2
            else:
                est = 0.001 + 0.0000005 * (float(N) ** 2) / 1.0e6
            max_evals = int(max(20, min(120, max_time / (est * 3.0 + 0.01))))
            a_final, true_final = true_line_search(
                best_a, is_lin, S, scale, max_evals, max_time, seed=int(seed) + 17
            )
            if math.isfinite(true_final) and true_final < best_true:
                best_true = true_final
                best_a = a_final.copy()
                print(f"Final line search improved to {best_true:.9f}")

    out = list_from_array(best_a)
    final_val = evaluate_safe(out)

    if not math.isfinite(final_val):
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 64:
            out = list_from_array(normalize_nonneg(parent))
            final_val = evaluate_safe(out)
        if not math.isfinite(final_val):
            out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
            final_val = evaluate_safe(out)

    print("Final returned score:", final_val, "length:", len(out))
    return out
```
